-----------------------------------------------------------------------------

The Taiwanese humpback dolphin is a subspecies of the Indo-Pacific humpback dolphin that is only found in a narrow stretch of water off the western coast of Taiwan.

Experts estimate that there are just over 60 of the animals left anywhere in the wild.

The groups are calling on the government to immediately order a halt of construction of the LNG terminal, as well as other projects in the area.
